# Assurance Governance Enforcement — A Assurance Model

Status: **A_DESIGN correction candidate — implementation forbidden until fresh exact-head design/assurance review is 0C/0M**

Base authority: merge commit `04e28eb7807acecc16161d6e33fb20bf88476ce7` (PR #19). Normative policy: `docs/architecture/ASSURANCE_MODEL_AND_PROOF_OBLIGATION_POLICY.md`. Functional plan: `docs/plans/assurance-proof-obligation-governance-enforcement.md`.

## Scope and implementation authority

This is code-bearing ABCP work. `documentation_only` is forbidden. B mutation authority is limited to these concrete paths/prefixes:

- `internal/context/**`
- `internal/governance/**`
- `internal/authority/**`
- `internal/ledger/**`
- `internal/integrationgate/**`
- `internal/githublifecycle/**`
- `internal/run/**`
- `internal/acceptance/**`
- `internal/authoritybackend/**`
- `internal/mergelifecycle/**`
- `internal/prlifecycle/**`
- `cmd/abcp/**`
- `scripts/acceptance/assurance-governance-dogfood.sh`
- `scripts/acceptance/assurance-governance-postmerge.sh`
- `scripts/acceptance/assurance-go-test-exact.py`
- `scripts/acceptance/assurance-graph-validate.py`
- `scripts/acceptance/assurance-lifecycle-integration.sh`
- `scripts/acceptance/assurance-runner-preflight.sh`
- `scripts/acceptance/assurance-secret-scan.py`
- `go.mod`
- `go.sum`
- `docs/plans/assurance-proof-obligation-governance-enforcement.md`
- `docs/plans/completed/assurance-proof-obligation-governance-enforcement.md`

`docs/plans/assurance-proof-obligation-governance-enforcement-assurance.md` is immutable throughout B/C. The functional plan may change only task checkbox/status markers and its final byte-identical semantic move to the named completed path; any other semantic plan edit is `MUTATION_SCOPE_VIOLATION`. Any need for another package/path is `SCOPE_EXPANSION_REQUIRED` and returns to A.

The two newly authorized compatibility paths are narrow: `internal/integrationgate/**` may only remove its clean-path `INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED→READY_FOR_MERGE` emission/return and preserve all earlier validation/failure behavior; `internal/githublifecycle/**` may only add (1) pre-READY `PRPublicationAuthorityV1` parsing/binding for PR publication and (2) post-PR `ReadyForMergeEvidenceV1`/`GitHubMergeAuthorityAdapterV1` parsing/binding into the existing EP-005 READY content/merge authority. Historical READY evidence and all existing TargetSubmission/merge predicates remain byte-compatible. No other package is needed for C1/M1–M8; the list above is the exact B mutation set, not a prefix from which sibling packages may be inferred.

Exactly three capsules remain authoritative: A owns this assurance model, B implements against it, and C independently executes branch-stage evidence and performs read-only exact-head review. No reviewer, validator, policy manifest, or evidence artifact is a fourth authority surface.

Finite cumulative final-review correction-reentry ceiling: **2 correction-B lineages per accepted A lineage**. One reentry is consumed only by the atomic durable controller-state CAS that publishes a new correction-B grant. A failed-C report may contain multiple mapped findings but consumes one reentry when one correction-B grant is published. Exact replay consumes zero additional reentries. After two successful correction-B grant publications, any later C implementation blocker returns to A.

## Frozen invariants

| ID | Invariant |
|---|---|
| AG-I01 | Successor assurance activation is authorized only from an exact existing merge/post-merge lifecycle authority for the policy commit, exact predecessor activation, authenticated acting principal, protected base branch, and strict controller-recomputed policy manifest. |
| AG-I02 | Every applicable failure scenario has stable referential traceability to invariant → proof obligation → one or more exact evidence cells. |
| AG-I03 | Assurance strengthens A/B/C without creating mutable reviewer authority or a fourth capsule. |
| AG-I04 | Work defaults code-bearing; any documentation-only exemption is controller-owned, path/semantic bounded, and exact-diff revalidated. |
| AG-I05 | Every evidence cell binds one proof obligation, one evidence class, one lifecycle stage, one exact subject rule, and one validator/artifact identity. |
| AG-I06 | B/C cannot weaken the frozen assurance model; model insufficiency returns to A as `ASSURANCE_MODEL_GAP`. |
| AG-I07 | C implementation findings invalidate C and may enter only bounded controller-derived correction-B authority from frozen A evidence. |
| AG-I08 | Assurance records are deterministic, strict, bounded, versioned, create-or-verify idempotent, and do not reinterpret historical V1/V2/V3 bytes. |
| AG-I09 | Fresh sessions fail closed when activation, model, proof, matrix, lineage, repository, backend, or exact-subject bindings are absent, stale, replaced, expired-by-state, or inconsistent. |
| AG-I10 | Every new durable authority publication uses durable content-addressed artifact → atomic controller-state CAS; crash/ambiguity cannot fork, reissue, or partially authorize state. |
| AG-I11 | Repository/controller/path/object identity cannot be substituted to obtain another lineage's assurance authority or evidence. |
| AG-I12 | Physical resources, retries, evidence, validator processes, execution time/output, and correction reentries are atomically reserved/charged and bounded cumulatively across the accepted A lineage. |
| AG-I13 | A→B, B→C, correction-B→replacement-C, integration, merge authorization, and post-merge derivations preserve the activated assurance digests and mandatory floors transitively. |
| AG-I14 | Cancellation, timeout, and external-observation ambiguity never create implicit retry authority; uncertain durable mutation is reconciled before another mutation attempt. |
| AG-I15 | Durable A/B/C grants and checkpoints have explicit state-based validity; they never silently expire by wall clock, while existing time-bounded execution/mutation leases remain unusable after their frozen expiry. |
| AG-I16 | Every validator attempt has one controller-issued reservation/ordinal, predecessor revision, secret-free execution policy, exact tool identity, and deterministic terminal evidence selection. |
| AG-I17 | Required integration acceptance is a merge-authorization prerequisite; any C invalidation races atomically with merge authority, and post-merge failure cannot be misreported as completion or silently rolled back. |
| AG-I18 | The production workflow-authority backend is a specified PostgreSQL linearizable CAS implementation with durable bootstrap, authenticated namespace, and fresh-process composition; backend absence/unavailability is fail-closed. |
| AG-I19 | Credential-bearing execution is partitioned from retained evidence: secret-capable children cannot expose credential bytes to tool subprocesses or retained raw output, and exact secret-value scanning gates every stage that used credentials. |
| AG-I20 | V4 lifecycle side effects linearize through controller-owned intent, exact ledger transition barrier, one-winner claim and separate durable call admission; PR/merge transport is impossible without the exact ephemeral single-consume capability and matching request digest. |
| AG-I21 | Every remote write effect has exactly one claim winner and at most one `CALL_POSSIBLE` admission; raw token is never durable, pre-admission loss is provably not applied, and after admission only read-only reconciliation is authorized. |
| AG-I22 | PostgreSQL enforces authority-domain isolation in the database itself with forced row-level security, direct-runtime and SECURITY DEFINER predicates derived from validated `session_user`, non-owner/no-bypass roles, fixed schema/search-path, and exact grants/revocations. |
| AG-I23 | Every digest-referenced governance/evidence ancestor is retrievable cross-host as a typed immutable blob, with distinct stage occurrence where required, from the same shared authority domain. |
| AG-I24 | The append-only run ledger remains operational run-state truth while PostgreSQL owns assurance/governance coordination; PR/merge claim and provider admission are protected by the exact ledger event/barrier and the frozen crash ordering never creates dual-authoritative state. |
| AG-I25 | V4 activation never migrates a live V3 workflow: one global predecessor directory and exclusive writer fence proves drain, tombstones later V1 CAS, preserves exact V1 bytes and starts V2 at predecessor revision + 1 in the same transaction with an empty grandfather set. |
| AG-I26 | PR publication is an external effect with the same intent/claim/reconciliation discipline as merge submission and cannot occur before integration acceptance. |
| AG-I27 | Correction-B paths are controller-derived only from the frozen semantic registry, validated finding rule IDs/affected paths, and the original B scope; reviewer/finding bytes cannot directly supply mutation paths. |
| AG-I28 | Secret-capable execution is bound in validator authority and every credential-bearing stage binds exact subject, controller secret-set commitment, candidate diff and sealed occurrence inventory into a zero-match scan before PASS. |
| AG-I29 | Resource profile/container capability is frozen in validator authority; shared atomic worker reservations cover process/container/tmpfs/shm/cache/log/image plus separately bounded Docker-daemon footprint across all workflows/controllers. |
| AG-I30 | J-05 delegation binds concrete floor/budget/provider/tool vectors and the full parent reservation plus exact toy plan/baseline; it authorizes child A only, and child A must mint its own repository-bound model before B/C. |
| AG-I31 | A remote effect is authorized only by one non-durable provider credential capability whose durable commitment is repeated by typed identity and repository-scope reads, the effect-specific request, claim/admission, mutation transport, result/reconciliation and APPLIED observation; GitHub App identity and installation repository scope are distinct facts and reusable credential bytes are never retained. |
| AG-I32 | A V4 effect barrier is ledger-enforced typed authority over one exact current source event and one byte-exact successor `ledger.Event`; event ID, timestamp, every provenance field, payload and evidence-reference byte are fixed before append, and no generic terminal append or untyped barrier removal can pass it. |
| AG-I33 | Every admitted PR/merge attempt has one locked durable attempt index. Result bytes and their index pointer commit atomically; only an indexed-result-absent CAS may create the unique response-free reconciliation anchor, followed by a strictly ordinal predecessor chain. |
| AG-I34 | Integration durably stops at `INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED`; pre-READY PR publication authority is complete without READY, and only exact PR APPLIED may derive the EP-005-compatible `READY_FOR_MERGE` evidence and retained merge authority. The dependency graph is acyclic. |
| AG-I35 | Forced PostgreSQL RLS remains effective inside all ten bytea SECURITY DEFINER functions through executable frozen function bodies, non-login/non-owner/non-bypass function roles and policies keyed to validated immutable `session_user`; no function relies on definer `current_user` as caller identity. |
| AG-I36 | Every ACTIVE artifact publisher has exact open/append/finalize/abandon authority under the stage-row lock, and cross-host crash recovery verifies signed, fresh, exact-owner process/cgroup/container absence before abandonment without admitting a late append. |
| AG-I37 | Every secret scan binds the exact stage-authorized diff base/candidate pair and its deterministic bytes; substitution of an empty, reversed, stale or unrelated diff cannot satisfy PASS. |
| AG-I38 | Each validator binds one complete flattened direct profile while each root reservation separately freezes its worker-charged envelope and direct allocation; child and sibling allocations consume only the envelope remainder in every dimension and can never be hidden by later parent growth. |
| AG-I39 | Every successor field, digest reference, absolute/relative path, database identity, array cardinality and cross-field/literal predicate has one extractable bounded descriptor and executable stable rejection mutation; catalog/vector generation fails closed on any untyped, unbounded, unstoreable or unenumerated authority. |

All minimum policy dimensions are applicable. No failure-model dimension is `not_applicable` for this controller-governance implementation. Evidence-class applicability is separately frozen below; `runtime` and `production` are explicitly N/A because this execution pack does not deploy a long-running production ABCP service.

## Frozen proof obligations

| ID | Falsifiable claim | Invariants |
|---|---|---|
| AG-PO-001 | `AssurancePolicyManifestV1` binds exact predecessor activation digest, exact authorized merge-result policy commit/tree, exact protected base branch, authenticated acting principal, successor capsule wire version `context-capsule-v4`, assurance schema `assurance-policy-v1`, and sorted canonical full policy path+SHA256 entries recomputed from that commit; no ambient/caller digest is trusted. | AG-I01,AG-I08,AG-I09 |
| AG-PO-002 | successor activation is permitted only when controller-derived `V3Drained` is true; the eligible in-flight predecessor set is therefore exactly empty. Any nonterminal V3 blocks activation, and after V4 activation all V3 records are historical evidence only. | AG-I01,AG-I08,AG-I09,AG-I25 |
| AG-PO-003 | successor activation installation is one atomic compare-and-swap over predecessor activation + expected controller revision; exact replay is idempotent and competing/conflicting installation changes no authority. | AG-I01,AG-I08,AG-I10 |
| AG-PO-004 | every assurance record strict-parses canonically; sorted-set reorder hashes identically while duplicate/unknown/ambiguous/oversized fields fail before authority/evidence allocation. | AG-I08,AG-I12 |
| AG-PO-005 | historical V1/V2/V3 bytes retain prior meaning and validators; V4 never reinterprets them. Because activation is drain-before-upgrade, no V3 operational continuation exists after V4 activation and historical V3 can never parent successor A/B/C. | AG-I01,AG-I08,AG-I25 |
| AG-PO-006 | every `AG-S-*`, `AG-I*`, `AG-PO-*`, evidence cell, validator, and journey reference resolves uniquely; every scenario reaches evidence and every proof obligation has ≥1 exact evidence cell. | AG-I02,AG-I05 |
| AG-PO-007 | every durable assurance blob is content-addressed/create-or-verify and every occurrence/authority record has a distinct immutable logical identity; conflicting bytes or identity reuse fails, unreferenced blobs grant no authority, and only the frozen occurrence/state CAS can make a child authoritative. | AG-I08,AG-I10 |
| AG-PO-008 | work defaults code-bearing; only controller-bound A classification can grant `documentation_only`, authoritative governance/policy is ineligible, and exact B/C diff semantic/path revalidation invalidates a false exemption. | AG-I04,AG-I09 |

| Finding | Frozen closure contract | Proof / evidence / validator |
|---|---|---|
| C1 mutation credential binding | `ProviderCredentialUseV1`, typed identity/scope read records and `ProviderAuthenticationContextV1` bind the exact non-durable mutation capability through request→admission→transport→result/reconciliation; app identity is not installation scope. | AG-PO-056 / AG-E-085 / AG-VAL-B-018 |
| M1 acyclic PR→READY authority | Pre-READY `PRPublicationAuthorityV1` seals PR bytes; PR APPLIED alone derives READY, then `GitHubMergeAuthorityAdapterV1` additively builds retained EP-005 merge authority. The frozen dependency graph has no back edge. | AG-PO-059 / AG-E-088 / AG-VAL-C-015 |
| M2 durable effect result index | `EffectAttemptIndexV1` and same-transaction artifact/index CAS make result presence/absence, anchor, reconciliation, settlement and resolution fresh-process discoverable at every crash boundary. | AG-PO-055,AG-PO-058 / AG-E-081,AG-E-087 / AG-VAL-C-014,AG-VAL-B-018 |
| M3 exact V4 ledger bytes | `EffectLedgerEventPreimageV1` freezes every inherited event field, deterministic ID/source+1ns timestamp, payload/evidence encoding and exact byte digest; typed barrier authority admits only those bytes. | AG-PO-057 / AG-E-086 / AG-VAL-B-019 |
| M4 executable FORCE-RLS functions | Literal create statements/bodies and request/result wires exist for all ten functions before ownership/grants; fresh bootstrap and drift verification execute them without B-authored semantics. | AG-PO-060 / AG-E-089 / AG-VAL-B-020 |
| M5 typed cross-host publisher absence | Registered Ed25519 worker observer, exact runtime owner, signed typed process/cgroup/container observations and locked freshness/order predicates are required before abandonment. | AG-PO-061 / AG-E-090 / AG-VAL-B-020 |
| M6 representable J-05 suballocation | Root envelope, explicit direct profile/counters and child allocation counters make direct+children/sibling inequalities executable; updated positive parent+child fixtures fit every dimension. | AG-PO-063 / AG-E-092 / AG-VAL-B-022 |
| M7 filesystem/DB identities | `path` and `absPath` are distinct; path identities use canonical absolute paths; authority domain and controller/worker/host identities exactly match DDL bounds with rejection vectors. | AG-PO-046,AG-PO-064 / AG-E-065,AG-E-093 / AG-VAL-B-014,AG-VAL-B-021 |
| M8 literal rejection generation | Exact literals are primitive descriptors plus `EXACT_LITERAL`; `CONSTANT_CHANGED` and the operator mutation table deterministically create a descriptor-valid target-predicate violation or extraction fails. | AG-PO-064 / AG-E-093 / AG-VAL-B-021 |

## Canonical successor wire contracts and validity predicates

This section is the sole V4 wire source of truth. All records use strict canonical JSON: UTF-8 only; no whitespace; object fields in the listed order; integers in the JSON integer grammar with no exponent/fraction; booleans lowercase; optional fields omitted, never `null`; and sets sorted by their stated key with no duplicate. Unknown/duplicate/missing required fields, invalid UTF-8, invalid enum, invalid lower-case digest/OID, over-bound value, trailing input, or a semantically false cross-field predicate fails before allocation or authority use. `sha256<T>` is exactly 64 lower-case hex characters and hashes the canonical preimage of `T`; `blob256` hashes exact bytes, not a JSON wrapper; `hexid` and `dbIdentity` are exactly 64 lower-case hex characters, with `hexid` assigned and `dbIdentity` the only wire type for PostgreSQL `char(64)` controller/worker/host identities; `gitOID` is exactly 40 lower-case hex characters. `id` is 1..128 bytes matching `[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:/-]*`; `identity` is 1..1024 bytes; `authorityDomain` is 1..128 ASCII bytes matching `[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}` and is the only wire type stored in PostgreSQL `authority_domain text`; `branch` is 1..255 UTF-8 bytes accepted by `git check-ref-format --branch`; `ref` is 6..1024 bytes, starts `refs/`, and passes `git check-ref-format`; `origin` is a normalized `https://host[:port]` value of 1..2048 bytes with no userinfo/path/query/fragment; `routeTemplate` is an absolute ASCII path template of 1..2048 bytes with only literal segments plus named `{field}` segments and no scheme/host/query/fragment; `route` is an absolute percent-escaped path of 1..2048 bytes with no scheme/host/query/fragment; `query` is 0..4096 ASCII bytes in canonical `url.Values.Encode` order; `prTitle` is 1..1024 UTF-8 bytes and `prBody` is 0..1024 UTF-8 bytes, both already trimmed, single-line and free of Unicode control code points. Repository-relative `path` is 1..1024 bytes, never begins `/`, and rejects empty/`.`/`..` segments, NUL, backslash, repeated slash, trailing slash and non-normal form. Canonical host `absPath` is 1..4096 bytes, begins exactly one `/`, is not `/`, and rejects NUL, backslash, empty/`.`/`..` segments, repeated/trailing slash and any value for which `filepath.Clean(value)!=value`; it never names a repository-relative path. `procStart` is decimal Linux `/proc/<pid>/stat` starttime ticks in `1..9223372036854775807`; `hex32` is exactly 32 lower-case hex bytes and `hex128` exactly 128 lower-case hex bytes. Ordinary `text` is 1..4096 bytes; `document` is 0..65536 bytes; command text is 1..8192 bytes; URL is 1..2048 bytes; `u64` is `0..9223372036854775807`. New timestamp/deadline fields are canonical UTC RFC3339 seconds exactly `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ`; `ledgerTimestamp` is the exact UTC string emitted by Go `time.Time.MarshalJSON` in RFC3339Nano form and must parse/re-emit byte-identically. Existing V1 fields retain their existing validators. An array written `[]T(order|set,min..max)` has that exact cardinality; no implicit array bound exists. Authority records are at most 1 MiB and retained blobs at most 32 MiB. A content digest is SHA-256 of the listed canonical fields; where a terminal self-digest is named, it is omitted from that preimage and appended last in the sealed form. A reference to `GovernanceActivationV1`, `ControllerStateV1`, `NextStageGrantV1`, `PhaseCheckpointV1`, `LifecycleBindingV1`, `ExecutionBoundsV1`, `ContextCapsuleV3`, `PhaseAuthorityV3`, `PhaseParentV1` or historical `ledger.Event` means its exact field order, preimage and validator at starting commit `9fc52f81008a98452a2a81c8a6b01bb6aa8c4783`; V4 event creation uses only the byte-exact successor preimage below and never calls the retained random/time constructor.

Every field token in the tables below resolves to `WireFieldDescriptorV1={schema_id:id,field_path:text<=512,ordinal:u64>=1,json_type:{STRING,INTEGER,BOOLEAN,OBJECT,ARRAY,RAW_JSON},value_type:text<=256,min_u64?:u64,max_u64?:u64,min_bytes?:u64,max_bytes?:u64,min_items?:u64,max_items?:u64,record_target?:id,digest_target?:id,optional:bool,literal_value?:text<=4096}`. An inline `field:type` annotation wins and recursively resolves the named primitive/record. A quoted or numeric exact scalar uses the underlying primitive's ordinary bounds and records the constant only in `literal_value` plus an `EXACT_LITERAL` predicate; it never narrows the primitive descriptor to a singleton. An exact boolean supplies BOOLEAN with `literal_value`. An exact literal string array supplies ARRAY with equal min/max cardinality and an element descriptor whose literal is separate. `?` alone means optional and never changes type. The only permitted bare scalars are resolved by this closed lexicon: `kind/schema_version` are `id` plus their row literal; the enum-name mapping in the next paragraph applies before fallback; `provider` and `model` are `id`; `base_branch` is `branch`; `authority_domain` is `authorityDomain`; `controller_identity`, `worker_identity`, `host_identity` and `observer_identity` are `dbIdentity`; `repository_identity`, `actor`, `acting_principal` and `classifier_identity` are `identity`; any other name ending `_identity` is `identity`; any other bare non-digest scalar is `text[1..4096]`. `PathIdentityV1.canonical_absolute_path`, every ledger/store/workdir/docker-root physical path and each cgroup filesystem path are `absPath`; policy/source/scope/plan paths are `path`. Bare arrays without `[]T(...)` or an exact literal array are forbidden. Bare `*_sha256` is forbidden unless its exact target appears in the row or the exhaustive digest-target registry below. Thus a field never inherits a JSON type, target or bound from an implementation convention.

Closed enums are exhaustive. `phase={A_DESIGN,B_IMPLEMENTATION,C_ACCEPTANCE_MERGE}`; `operation={design-planning,design-review,implementation,implementation-review,acceptance,final-review,pr-publication,merge-authorization,post-merge-acceptance,deployment,recovery,maintenance,dogfood-delegation}`; `review_profile={NONE,INITIAL_IMPLEMENTATION,CORRECTION}`; evidence stage `{B_IMPLEMENTATION,C_BRANCH_ACCEPTANCE,INTEGRATION_ACCEPTANCE,POST_MERGE_ACCEPTANCE}`; checkpoint kind `{DESIGN_ACCEPTED,IMPLEMENTATION_CONVERGED,ACCEPTANCE_PASSED,FINAL_REVIEW_CLEAN,INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED,PR_PUBLISHED,MERGE_AUTHORIZED,MERGE_APPLIED,POST_MERGE_ACCEPTED,POST_MERGE_FAILED}`; work class `{CODE_BEARING,DOCUMENTATION_ONLY}`; finding class `{IMPLEMENTATION_FINDING,ASSURANCE_MODEL_GAP}`; correction relation `{IMPLEMENTATION_CORRECTABLE,A_REQUIRED}`; secret capability `{NONE,POSTGRES,CODEX_PROVIDER,DOCKER_PASSWORD}`; network policy `{DENY_ALL,LOOPBACK_BROKER,LOOPBACK_POSTGRES,DOCKER_SOCKET}`; evidence/occurrence outcome `{PASS,FAIL,VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE,UNSELECTED,ORPHAN}`; artifact kind `{AUTHORITY,EVIDENCE,STDOUT,STDERR,STRUCTURED_RESULT,RESOURCE_EVIDENCE,CANDIDATE_DIFF,SECRET_SCAN,INVENTORY_PAGE,INVENTORY_MANIFEST,INCIDENT,PREDECESSOR_SNAPSHOT,PROVIDER_OBSERVATION}`; coordination state `{NONE,DESIGN_ACCEPTED,IMPLEMENTATION_CONVERGED,BRANCH_ACCEPTED,FINAL_REVIEW_CLEAN,INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED,PR_SUBMITTING,PR_NOT_APPLIED,PR_PUBLISHED,MERGE_AUTHORIZED,MERGE_SUBMITTING,MERGE_APPLIED,MERGE_NOT_APPLIED,POST_MERGE_ACCEPTED,POST_MERGE_FAILED,RECOVERY_REQUIRED}`; effect kind `{PR,MERGE}`; effect attempt state `{NONE,INTENT_DURABLE,CLAIMED_PRE_CALL,CALL_POSSIBLE,APPLIED,NOT_APPLIED,UNKNOWN,RECOVERY_REQUIRED}`; provider disposition `{APPLIED,NOT_APPLIED,UNKNOWN}`; provider observation source `{MUTATION_RESPONSE,READ_ONLY_RECONCILIATION}`; barrier action `{RESOLVE,RETAIN}`; barrier resolution reason `{UNCLAIMED_ABANDONMENT,SETTLED_APPLIED,SETTLED_NOT_APPLIED}`; reservation lifecycle `{RESERVED,RUNNING,SETTLING,RELEASED,RECOVERY_HOLD}`; publisher lifecycle `{ACTIVE,FINALIZED,ABANDONED}`; stage seal state `{OPEN,CLOSED}`; cleanup/release outcome `{CLEAN,ABSENT_PROVED,RECOVERY_HOLD}`; recovery outcome `{APPLIED,NOT_APPLIED,UNKNOWN,RECOVERY_REQUIRED}`; post-merge reason `{POST_MERGE_VALIDATOR_FAILED,POST_MERGE_VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE,POST_MERGE_SUBJECT_MISMATCH,POST_MERGE_SECRET_SCAN_FAILED}`; merge method `{merge,rebase,squash}`; HTTP method `{GET,POST,PATCH}`; crash family `{ARTIFACT,CORRECTION,CUTOVER,MERGE_EFFECT,POST_MERGE,PR_EFFECT,RESOURCE,STAGE}`; crash boundary `{BEFORE_DURABLE_CHILD,AFTER_DURABLE_CHILD_BEFORE_CAS,CAS_RESPONSE_LOST,AFTER_BARRIER_BEFORE_CLAIM,AFTER_CLAIM_BEFORE_ADMISSION,ADMISSION_RESPONSE_LOST,AFTER_ADMISSION_BEFORE_RESULT,AFTER_RESULT_BEFORE_SETTLEMENT,AFTER_LEDGER_BEFORE_PG,AFTER_PG_BEFORE_BARRIER_RESOLVE}`. Ledger run states are the exact existing `domain.State` strings; PR intents admit only an event whose `state_to` is exactly `INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED`, and merge intents only `READY_FOR_MERGE`.

Additional closed enums are: provider principal type `{GITHUB_USER,GITHUB_APP_INSTALLATION}`; provider credential kind `{GITHUB_USER_TOKEN,GITHUB_APP_JWT,GITHUB_INSTALLATION_TOKEN}`; credential role `{MUTATION,APP_IDENTITY}`; provider read purpose `{USER_IDENTITY,APP_IDENTITY,INSTALLATION_IDENTITY,USER_REPOSITORY_ACCESS,INSTALLATION_REPOSITORIES,PR_RECONCILIATION,REF_RECONCILIATION,COMMIT_RECONCILIATION}`; provider parser `{GITHUB_USER_V1,GITHUB_APP_V1,GITHUB_INSTALLATION_V1,GITHUB_REPOSITORY_V1,GITHUB_INSTALLATION_REPOSITORIES_PAGE_V1,GITHUB_PULL_REQUEST_V1,GITHUB_PULL_REQUEST_PAGE_V1,GITHUB_GIT_REF_V1,GITHUB_GIT_COMMIT_V1}`; repository permission `{READ,TRIAGE,WRITE,MAINTAIN,ADMIN}`; PR mutation mode `{CREATE,UPDATE}`; ledger effect outcome `{APPLIED,NOT_APPLIED,UNCLAIMED}`; barrier proof kind `{EXACT_SETTLEMENT,UNCLAIMED_ABANDONMENT}`; publisher terminal operation `{FINALIZE,ABANDON}`; process absence disposition `{PID_ABSENT,PID_REUSED}`; container absence disposition `{CONTAINER_ABSENT}`; cgroup absence disposition `{CGROUP_UNPOPULATED}`; observer probe `{PROCFS_PID_STARTTIME_V1,CGROUP_V2_EVENTS_V1,DOCKER_INSPECT_NOT_FOUND_V1}`; resource composition `{FLATTENED_SUM_V1,INCLUSIVE_PARENT_SUBALLOCATION_V1}`. The retained `[]T(set)`/`[]T(order)` spelling expands exactly to cardinality `0..256`; any nonempty, exact-empty or narrower requirement is one of the stable predicates below.

Field use selects exactly one of those enums: capsule/authority/parent/grant/checkpoint/failed-stage/escape `stage` is `phase`; subject, artifact, secret, evidence, resource and closure `stage` is evidence stage; `classification` is work class only in `WorkClassificationV1` and finding class in finding/failure records; `outcome` is evidence/occurrence outcome unless a record fixes a literal; `kind` in `PhaseCheckpointV2` is checkpoint kind. Bare `operation`, `review_profile`, `effect_kind`, `method`, `principal_type`, `credential_kind`, `credential_role`, `read_purpose`, `parser`, `repository_permission`, `mutation_mode`, `merge_method`, `observation_source`, `proof_kind`, `resolution_reason`, `barrier_action`, `accounting_mode`, `composition`, `secret_capability`, `network_policy`, `artifact_kind`, `evidence_class`, `cleanup_outcome`, `recovery_outcome`, `family`, `boundary`, `correction_relation`, `binding_kind`, `lease_state`, `writer_state`, `scan_outcome`, `closure_outcome`, `next_coordination_state`, `settled_disposition`, `outcome_disposition`, `process_disposition`, `cgroup_disposition`, `container_disposition` and `probe_method` map respectively to their same-purpose closed enum above; `disposition` is provider disposition in effect rows and the retained input-head enum in inherited rows; `lifecycle` is reservation or publisher lifecycle according to its record. Any same-spelled field with more than one possible enum must be selected by the record row or extraction fails. Checkpoint verdict is fixed by kind as `{DESIGN_ACCEPTED:DESIGN_ACCEPTED,IMPLEMENTATION_CONVERGED:IMPLEMENTATION_CONVERGED_C0_M0,ACCEPTANCE_PASSED:ACCEPTANCE_PASSED,FINAL_REVIEW_CLEAN:FINAL_REVIEW_CLEAN_C0_M0,INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED:INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED,PR_PUBLISHED:PR_PUBLISHED,MERGE_AUTHORIZED:MERGE_AUTHORIZED,MERGE_APPLIED:MERGE_APPLIED,POST_MERGE_ACCEPTED:POST_MERGE_ACCEPTED,POST_MERGE_FAILED:POST_MERGE_FAILED}`; the exact current V1 kind/operation/floor rules remain mandatory for their seven inherited kinds.

Except where an inherited V1 record or a row states otherwise, every top-level record's `kind` literal is its exact backticked record name and its `schema_version` literal is the lower-case identifier after `/` in its table row. Nested records have no implicit wrapper fields. This convention is part of every preimage and vector.

`FailureDimension` is exactly `{AUTHORITY_IDENTITY,CANCELLATION_AMBIGUITY,COMPATIBILITY_MIGRATION_VERSION_SKEW,CONCURRENCY_RACES,CRASH_CONSISTENCY,EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY,FILESYSTEM_IDENTITY,INTEGRATION_E2E,MALFORMED_FORGED_INPUT,PARTIAL_FAILURE,PHYSICAL_RESOURCE_EXHAUSTION,RESTART_RECOVERY_ROLLBACK,RETRY_REPLAY_IDEMPOTENCY,SECURITY_PRIVACY_BOUNDARY}`. `EvidenceClass` is exactly `{contract,crash_restart,e2e,fault_injection,integration,migration,race_concurrency,replay_idempotency,resource,security_negative,smoke,static,unit}`. Scan/closure outcomes use `{PASS,FAIL,VALIDATION_UNAVAILABLE}`. Predecessor writer state is `{OPEN,TOMBSTONED}` and predecessor binding kind is `{WORKFLOW_STATE,LEDGER,PR_ADMISSION_STORE,MERGE_STATE_STORE}`.

`b64MiB` is padded RFC4648 base64 of 1..1048576 decoded bytes, 4..1398104 ASCII bytes, with decode→encode byte equality; it exists only for exact bytea function request fields below.

`timestamp` is the explicit primitive name for the canonical UTC-second form in the preceding paragraph; every bare `*_at`, `deadline`, `valid_from` and `valid_through` field resolves to it unless its row explicitly selects `ledgerTimestamp`. The `absPath` clean check is exactly Go 1.23 `path/filepath.Clean` with `GOOS=linux`; non-Linux execution cannot mint or validate V4 host-path authority.

### Authority and lineage records

| Record / schema | Exact canonical fields and predicates |
|---|---|
| `PolicyUpgradeAuthorityV1` / `policy-upgrade-authority-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,base_branch,expected_premerge_base_oid:gitOID,reviewed_head_oid:gitOID,merge_result_oid:gitOID,merge_result_tree_oid:gitOID,merge_method,acting_principal,merge_authorized_checkpoint_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV1>,post_merge_accepted_checkpoint_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV1>,provider_evidence_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,policy_evidence_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,predecessor_activation_sha256:sha256<GovernanceActivationV1>`; the two checkpoints are exact predecessor V3 governance of the V4 policy merge, avoiding any successor self-authorization. |
| `WorkClassificationV1` / `work-classification-v1` | `kind,schema_version,classification,eligible_paths:[]path(set),rationale,classifier_identity` |
| `GovernanceActivationV2` / `governance-activation-v2` | `kind,schema_version,policy_version="context-capsule-v4",predecessor_activation_sha256:sha256<GovernanceActivationV1>,policy_manifest_sha256:sha256<AssurancePolicyManifestV1>,policy_upgrade_authority_sha256:sha256<PolicyUpgradeAuthorityV1>,activation_repository_commit:gitOID,activation_repository_tree:gitOID,activation_sequence:u64>=1,predecessor_state_v1_artifact_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,predecessor_state_v1_revision:u64>=1,predecessor_cutover_sha256:sha256<PredecessorCutoverV1>,grandfathered_v3:[]sha256<ContextCapsuleV3>(set)`; the last set is exactly empty. |
| `PredecessorAuthorityDirectoryV1` / `predecessor-authority-directory-v1` | `kind,schema_version,authority_domain,controller_identity,repository_identity,writer_epoch:u64>=1,writer_state,bindings:[]PredecessorStoreBindingV1(set:binding_id,1..256),active_writer_leases:[]sha256<PredecessorWriterLeaseV1>(set),directory_revision:u64>=1,directory_sha256:self`; completeness requires exactly one `WORKFLOW_STATE`, at least one `LEDGER` for every bound run, and the unique configured PR/merge roots used by any registered writer. |
| `PredecessorWriterLeaseV1` / `predecessor-writer-lease-v1` | `kind,schema_version,lease_id,authority_domain,controller_identity,repository_identity,writer_epoch:u64,binding_ids:[]id(set),operation,owner_instance_sha256:sha256<ExecutionIdentityV1>,opened_at,deadline,lease_state`; `lease_state={ACTIVE,RELEASED}`, deadlines are canonical RFC3339 seconds, and every V1 workflow CAS/ledger transition/PR or merge provider admission must hold an ACTIVE lease plus the shared cutover lock. |
| `PredecessorCutoverV1` / `predecessor-cutover-v1` | `kind,schema_version,authority_domain,controller_identity,repository_identity,directory_sha256:sha256<PredecessorAuthorityDirectoryV1>,writer_epoch:u64,linearization_revision:u64,predecessor_state_v1_revision:u64,predecessor_state_v1_sha256:blob256,predecessor_state_v1_artifact_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,barrier_snapshot_sha256s:[]sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>(set),v2_revision:u64,tombstone_state="TOMBSTONED",cutover_sha256:self`; `v2_revision=predecessor_state_v1_revision+1` and all fields are written by one transaction. |
| `AssurancePolicyManifestV1` / `assurance-policy-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,activation_repository_commit:gitOID,activation_repository_tree:gitOID,predecessor_activation_sha256:sha256<GovernanceActivationV1>,lifecycle_authority_sha256:sha256<PolicyUpgradeAuthorityV1>,acting_principal,capsule_policy_version="context-capsule-v4",assurance_schema_version="assurance-model-v1",policy_files:[]PolicyFileV1(set:path)` |
| `FinalReviewProfileV1` / `final-review-profile-v1` | `kind,schema_version,profile_id,provider,model,reasoning_effort,fresh_session:bool,required_critical:u64=0,required_major:u64=0,classification_vocabulary:[]finding_class(order)` |
| `SemanticAuthorityRegistryV2` / `semantic-authority-registry-v2` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,accepted_a_head:gitOID,component_scopes:[]ComponentScopeV1(set:id),rules:[]SemanticRuleV2(set:rule_id),final_review_profile_sha256:sha256<FinalReviewProfileV1>` |
| `AssuranceModelV1` / `assurance-model-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,accepted_a_head:gitOID,policy_manifest_sha256:sha256<AssurancePolicyManifestV1>,semantic_registry_sha256:sha256<SemanticAuthorityRegistryV2>,work_classification_sha256:sha256<WorkClassificationV1>,final_review_profile_sha256:sha256<FinalReviewProfileV1>,proof_obligation_set_sha256:sha256<ProofObligationSetV1>,evidence_matrix_sha256:sha256<EvidenceMatrixV1>,resource_profile_set_sha256:sha256<ResourceProfileSetV1>,canonical_vector_catalog_sha256:sha256<CanonicalVectorCatalogV1>,failure_scenarios:[]ScenarioV1(set:id),invariants:[]InvariantV1(set:id),proof_obligations:[]ProofV1(set:id),validators:[]ValidatorV1(set:id),evidence_cells:[]CellV1(set:id),journeys:[]JourneyV1(set:id),resource_profiles:[]ResourceProfileV1(set:id),materialized_resource_profiles:[]MaterializedResourceProfileV1(set:id),correction_reentry_limit:u64=2,allowed_b_paths:[]path(set)`; all four independent projections are recomputed from these exact nested values. |
| `ProofObligationSetV1` / `proof-obligation-set-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,accepted_a_head:gitOID,proof_obligations:[]ProofV1(set:id)` |
| `EvidenceMatrixV1` / `evidence-matrix-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,accepted_a_head:gitOID,validators:[]ValidatorV1(set:id),evidence_cells:[]CellV1(set:id),journeys:[]JourneyV1(set:id)` |
| `ResourceProfileSetV1` / `resource-profile-set-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,accepted_a_head:gitOID,resource_profiles:[]ResourceProfileV1(set:id),materialized_resource_profiles:[]MaterializedResourceProfileV1(set:id),lineage_budget_sha256:sha256<ResourceBudgetV1>,worker_capacity_sha256:sha256<WorkerCapacityV1>,docker_daemon_capacity_sha256:sha256<DockerDaemonCapacityV1>`; every validator profile ID/digest resolves exactly once in `materialized_resource_profiles`. |
| `AcceptedALineageV1` / `accepted-a-lineage-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity,a_capsule_sha256:sha256<ContextCapsuleV4>,design_accepted_checkpoint_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,a_to_b_grant_sha256:sha256<StageGrantV2>,policy_manifest_sha256,assurance_model_sha256,semantic_registry_sha256,proof_obligation_set_sha256,evidence_matrix_sha256,resource_profile_set_sha256,canonical_vector_catalog_sha256,work_classification_sha256,final_review_profile_sha256`; every digest is typed by the same-named record above and must equal its capsule/checkpoint/grant value. |
| `PhaseParentV2` / `phase-parent-v2` | Exact V3 `PhaseParentV1` fields retained in order: `capsule_file_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,capsule_sha256:sha256<ContextCapsuleV4>,stage,checkpoint_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,candidate_sha:gitOID,grant_sha256:sha256<StageGrantV2>`; then additive `failed_stage_sha256?:sha256<FailedStageV1>,correction_grant_sha256?:sha256<CorrectionBGrantV1>`. Both additive fields occur together only for correction B/replacement C. |
| `PhaseAuthorityV4` / `phase-authority-v4` | Exact V3 `PhaseAuthorityV3` fields retained in order: `stage,allowed_operations:[]operation(set),parent?:PhaseParentV2,semantic_registry_sha256,observation_scope_ids:[]id(set),blocking_scope_ids:[]id(set),mutation_scope_ids:[]id(set),authorized_finding_ids:[]id(set),authorized_invariant_ids:[]id(set),allowed_paths:[]path(set),review_profile,execution_bounds?:ExecutionBoundsV1`; then additive `policy_manifest_sha256,assurance_model_sha256,proof_obligation_set_sha256,evidence_matrix_sha256,resource_profile_set_sha256,canonical_vector_catalog_sha256,work_classification_sha256,final_review_profile_sha256,correction_reentry_limit:u64=2`. V3 subset/floor and stage/profile predicates remain mandatory. |
| `ContextCapsuleV4` / `context-capsule-v4` | Additive V3 capsule, exact order: `policy_version="context-capsule-v4",project,plan,roadmap_phase,execution_pack,task,operation_context?:OperationContextV1,phase_authority:PhaseAuthorityV4,repository,base_sha:gitOID,invariants:[]text(set),non_goals:[]text(set),predecessor_outcomes:[]OutcomeV1(set:task),sources:[]SourceV1(set:path)`; then `repository_identity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256?:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,policy_manifest_sha256,assurance_model_sha256,semantic_registry_sha256,proof_obligation_set_sha256,evidence_matrix_sha256,resource_profile_set_sha256,canonical_vector_catalog_sha256,work_classification_sha256,final_review_profile_sha256,capsule_sha256:self`. The lineage field is absent only on the root A capsule and mandatory/equal thereafter. No existing V3 field is removed or weakened. |
| `PhaseCheckpointV2` / `phase-checkpoint-v2` | Additive V1 checkpoint, exact order: `kind,repository,capsule_file_sha256,capsule_sha256,candidate_sha:gitOID,operation,verdict,evidence:[]EvidenceBindingV1(set:ref),controller_policy_identity,sequence:u64,predecessor_checkpoint_sha256?:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,controller_event_identity,semantic_registry_sha256,next_stage_grant_sha256?:sha256<StageGrantV2>,review_scope_tip_sha256?:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,acceptance_result_sha256?:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,review_critical:u64,review_major:u64,reviewed_blocking_scope_ids:[]id(set),lifecycle?:LifecycleBindingV1`; then `schema_version,repository_identity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256?:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,stage,candidate_tree_oid:gitOID,ledger_event_sha256?:sha256<LedgerEventBindingV1>,policy_manifest_sha256,assurance_model_sha256,proof_obligation_set_sha256,evidence_matrix_sha256,resource_profile_set_sha256,canonical_vector_catalog_sha256,work_classification_sha256,final_review_profile_sha256,evidence_selection_sha256?:sha256<StageEvidenceSelectionV1>,checkpoint_sha256:self`. Lineage is absent only on the first `DESIGN_ACCEPTED` checkpoint and mandatory thereafter; ledger event is absent before integration and required from `INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED` onward. Existing checkpoint kind/operation/verdict/evidence/lifecycle predicates are floors. |
| `StageGrantV2` / `stage-grant-v2` | Additive `NextStageGrantV1`, exact order: `kind,stage,parent_checkpoint_sha256:sha256<CheckpointGrantParentV2>,base_sha:gitOID,semantic_registry_sha256,allowed_operations:[]operation(set),observation_scope_ids:[]id(set),blocking_scope_ids:[]id(set),mutation_scope_ids:[]id(set),authorized_finding_ids:[]id(set),authorized_invariant_ids:[]id(set),allowed_paths:[]path(set),review_profile,execution_bounds?:ExecutionBoundsV1,required_blocking_scope_ids:[]id(set),required_invariant_ids:[]id(set),required_operations:[]operation(set),required_final_review_ids:[]id(set)`; then `schema_version,repository_identity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256?:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,policy_manifest_sha256,assurance_model_sha256,proof_obligation_set_sha256,evidence_matrix_sha256,resource_profile_set_sha256,canonical_vector_catalog_sha256,work_classification_sha256,final_review_profile_sha256,remaining_resource_budget_sha256:sha256<ResourceBudgetV1>,correction_reentry_limit:u64=2,correction_reentries_used:u64<=2,failed_stage_sha256?:sha256<FailedStageV1>,correction_grant_sha256?:sha256<CorrectionBGrantV1>,grant_sha256:self`. Lineage is absent only on the initial A→B grant and mandatory thereafter. All four V1 required-floor sets are non-empty, retained and only narrow through descendants. |

### Correction, evidence, artifact, secret and resource records

| Record / schema | Exact canonical fields and predicates |
|---|---|
| `FindingEvidenceV2` / `finding-evidence-v2` | `kind,schema_version,finding_id,rule_id,classification,affected_path_set_sha256:sha256<ControllerAffectedPathSetV1>,evidence_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,review_report_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>` |
| `ControllerAffectedPathSetV1` / `controller-affected-path-set-v1` | `kind,schema_version,finding_id,base_oid:gitOID,candidate_oid:gitOID,changed_paths:[]path(set),rule_scope_paths:[]path(set),original_b_paths:[]path(set),derived_affected_paths:[]path(set),derivation="GIT_DIFF_RULE_SCOPE_INTERSECTION_V1"` |
| `IntegrationFailureV1` / `integration-failure-v1` | `kind,schema_version,accepted_a_lineage_sha256,c_stage_tip_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,integration_subject_sha256:sha256<StageSubjectV1>,integration_validator_id,classification,finding_ids:[]id(set),evidence_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,created_sequence:u64>=1` |
| `FailedStageV1` / `failed-stage-v1` | `kind,schema_version,accepted_a_lineage_sha256,stage,candidate_oid:gitOID,candidate_tree_oid:gitOID,failed_capsule_sha256:sha256<ContextCapsuleV4>,stage_tip_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,classification,finding_ids:[]id(set),evidence_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,integration_failure_sha256?:sha256<IntegrationFailureV1>,invalidated_tip_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,created_sequence:u64>=1`; it contains no paths. |
| `CorrectionBGrantV1` / `correction-b-grant-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity:identity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,failed_stage_sha256:sha256<FailedStageV1>,failed_c_capsule_sha256:sha256<ContextCapsuleV4>,prior_c_tip_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,original_b_grant_sha256:sha256<StageGrantV2>,correction_generation:u64>=1,correction_reentry_ordinal:u64=1..2,finding_ids:[]id(set),affected_path_sets:[]sha256<ControllerAffectedPathSetV1>(set),derived_allowed_paths:[]path(set),allowed_operations:[]operation(set),observation_scope_ids:[]id(set),blocking_scope_ids:[]id(set),mutation_scope_ids:[]id(set),authorized_invariant_ids:[]id(set),required_blocking_scope_ids:[]id(set),required_invariant_ids:[]id(set),required_operations:[]operation(set),required_final_review_ids:[]id(set),policy_manifest_sha256:sha256<AssurancePolicyManifestV1>,assurance_model_sha256:sha256<AssuranceModelV1>,semantic_registry_sha256:sha256<SemanticAuthorityRegistryV2>,proof_obligation_set_sha256:sha256<ProofObligationSetV1>,evidence_matrix_sha256:sha256<EvidenceMatrixV1>,resource_profile_set_sha256:sha256<ResourceProfileSetV1>,canonical_vector_catalog_sha256:sha256<CanonicalVectorCatalogV1>,work_classification_sha256:sha256<WorkClassificationV1>,final_review_profile_sha256:sha256<FinalReviewProfileV1>,original_b_scope_sha256:sha256<PathSetV1>,execution_bounds_sha256:sha256<ExecutionBoundsV1>,remaining_resource_budget_sha256:sha256<ResourceBudgetV1>,grant_sha256:self`; all assurance digests/floors equal original B and paths/bounds only narrow. |
| `AssuranceEscapeV1` / `assurance-escape-v1` | `kind,schema_version,accepted_a_lineage_sha256,discovery_stage,candidate_oid:gitOID,missed_scenario_ids:[]id(set),affected_invariant_ids:[]id(set),affected_proof_ids:[]id(set),evidence_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,required_action="A_REQUIRED"` |
| `PathSetV1` / `path-set-v1` | `kind,schema_version,paths:[]path(set)` |
| `StageSubjectV1` / `stage-subject-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity:identity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,stage,candidate_oid:gitOID,candidate_tree_oid:gitOID,diff_base_oid:gitOID,diff_candidate_oid:gitOID,stage_generation:u64>=1,predecessor_stage_tip_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,diff_lineage_sha256:sha256<StageDiffLineageV1>,integration_subject_sha256?:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,merge_result_oid?:gitOID,merge_result_tree_oid?:gitOID`; `P-SUBJECT-001` requires `diff_candidate_oid=candidate_oid`; optional integration/merge pairs are present iff their stage; `P-SUBJECT-002` requires every field to equal the stage-specific `StageDiffLineageV1`. |
| `StageDiffLineageV1` / `stage-diff-lineage-v1` | `kind,schema_version,repository_identity:identity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,stage,stage_subject_preimage_sha256:blob256,base_oid:gitOID,candidate_oid:gitOID,derivation_authority_sha256:sha256<StageGrantV2-or-PhaseCheckpointV2>,derivation_rule:{B_GRANT_BASE_TO_B_CANDIDATE,C_GRANT_BASE_TO_UNCHANGED_C_CANDIDATE,INTEGRATION_BASE_TO_INTEGRATED_HEAD,PREMERGE_BASE_TO_MERGE_RESULT}`; `P-DIFF-001` selects exactly the listed rule by stage; `P-DIFF-002` requires B base from the active B grant, C base from its transitively preserved B base and candidate equal the accepted C head, integration base/candidate from exact integration input/result, and post-merge base/result from exact merge settlement; `stage_subject_preimage_sha256` hashes the StageSubject fields through `predecessor_stage_tip_sha256`, avoiding a cycle. |
| `ArtifactBlobV1` / `artifact-blob-v1` | Content identity is `artifact_sha256=blob256(canonical_bytes)`; metadata is `byte_size:u64=1..33554432`. Blob identity is independent of lineage, stage, occurrence and kind; create-or-verify compares exact digest/size/bytes. |
| `ArtifactOccurrenceV1` / `artifact-occurrence-v1` | `kind,schema_version,occurrence_id:hexid,authority_domain,accepted_a_lineage_sha256,stage,stage_subject_sha256:sha256<StageSubjectV1>,occurrence_ordinal:u64>=1,publisher_reservation_sha256:sha256<ArtifactPublisherReservationV1>,artifact_sha256:sha256<ArtifactBlobV1>,artifact_kind,outcome,byte_size:u64,created_sequence:u64>=1`; uniqueness is `(domain,lineage,stage,subject,ordinal)` and `occurrence_id`, so identical blobs may occur repeatedly. |
| `WorkerObservationKeyV1` / `worker-observation-key-v1` | `kind,schema_version,worker_identity:dbIdentity,host_identity:dbIdentity,observer_identity:dbIdentity,key_id:hexid,algorithm="ED25519",public_key_hex:hex32,valid_from,valid_through,registration_revision:u64>=1,key_sha256:self`; `observer_identity=hex(SHA256("ABCP-WORKER-OBSERVER-V1" || NUL || worker_identity || NUL || host_identity || NUL || key_id))`. The key is controller-registered before an owner starts and cannot rotate while an ACTIVE publisher names it. |
| `RuntimeOwnerIdentityV1` / `runtime-owner-identity-v1` | `kind,schema_version,worker_identity:dbIdentity,host_identity:dbIdentity,pid:u64=1..4194304,process_start_ticks:procStart,boot_id:hex32,cgroup_path:absPath,cgroup_inode:u64>=1,container_id?:hexid,docker_root_identity_sha256?:sha256<PathIdentityV1>,observer_key_sha256:sha256<WorkerObservationKeyV1>,started_at,owner_sha256:self`; container fields occur together; `(host_identity,boot_id,pid,process_start_ticks)` is the process identity and cgroup/container fields are immutable for its execution. |
| `ProcessAbsenceObservationV1` / `process-absence-observation-v1` | `kind,schema_version,owner_sha256:sha256<RuntimeOwnerIdentityV1>,worker_identity:dbIdentity,host_identity:dbIdentity,observer_identity:dbIdentity,observer_key_sha256:sha256<WorkerObservationKeyV1>,observed_at,probe_method="PROCFS_PID_STARTTIME_V1",proc_stat_path:absPath,expected_pid:u64=1..4194304,expected_start_ticks:procStart,observed_start_ticks?:procStart,process_disposition,probe_result_sha256:blob256,signature_hex:hex128`; `proc_stat_path` is exactly `/proc/` + decimal `expected_pid` + `/stat`. PID_ABSENT requires kernel ENOENT and omits observed start; PID_REUSED reads at most 4,096 exact stat bytes, requires a different parsed field-22 start tick, and rejects truncation/parse error. The result preimage is exact no-whitespace JSON `{"probe_method":"PROCFS_PID_STARTTIME_V1","path":<JSON path>,"expected_start_ticks":<JSON decimal string>,"result":"PID_ABSENT"}` or the same field order plus `,"observed_start_ticks":<JSON decimal string>` before `result="PID_REUSED"`; `probe_result_sha256` hashes those bytes. The signature is `hex(Ed25519.Sign(private_key,SHA256(UTF8("ABCP-PROCESS-ABSENCE-V1") || NUL || unsigned_canonical_bytes)))`, where `unsigned_canonical_bytes` are this record's exact fields through `probe_result_sha256`. |
| `CgroupAbsenceObservationV1` / `cgroup-absence-observation-v1` | `kind,schema_version,owner_sha256:sha256<RuntimeOwnerIdentityV1>,worker_identity:dbIdentity,host_identity:dbIdentity,observer_identity:dbIdentity,observer_key_sha256:sha256<WorkerObservationKeyV1>,observed_at,probe_method="CGROUP_V2_EVENTS_V1",cgroup_path:absPath,cgroup_inode:u64>=1,populated:bool=false,cgroup_disposition="CGROUP_UNPOPULATED",probe_result_sha256:blob256,signature_hex:hex128`; path/inode equal the owner; the observer reads at most 4,096 exact `<cgroup_path>/cgroup.events` bytes, rejects truncation/duplicate/malformed keys and requires exactly one parsed `populated 0`. The result preimage is exact no-whitespace JSON `{"probe_method":"CGROUP_V2_EVENTS_V1","path":<JSON path>,"inode":<u64>,"populated":false}` and `probe_result_sha256` hashes those bytes. The signature uses the same formula with domain `ABCP-CGROUP-ABSENCE-V1`. |
| `ContainerAbsenceObservationV1` / `container-absence-observation-v1` | `kind,schema_version,owner_sha256:sha256<RuntimeOwnerIdentityV1>,worker_identity:dbIdentity,host_identity:dbIdentity,observer_identity:dbIdentity,observer_key_sha256:sha256<WorkerObservationKeyV1>,observed_at,probe_method="DOCKER_INSPECT_NOT_FOUND_V1",container_id:hexid,docker_root_identity_sha256:sha256<PathIdentityV1>,container_disposition="CONTAINER_ABSENT",probe_result_sha256:blob256,signature_hex:hex128`; container/root equal the owner. The registered worker observer performs Docker Engine `GET /containers/<container_id>/json`; only HTTP 404 with a bounded ≤4,096-byte JSON error body is not-found, while daemon unavailable/permission/other status/malformed body is not absence. The result preimage is exact no-whitespace JSON `{"probe_method":"DOCKER_INSPECT_NOT_FOUND_V1","container_id":<JSON id>,"http_status":404,"result":"CONTAINER_ABSENT"}` and `probe_result_sha256` hashes those bytes. The signature formula uses domain `ABCP-CONTAINER-ABSENCE-V1`. |
| `ArtifactPublisherReservationV1` / `artifact-publisher-reservation-v1` | `kind,schema_version,publisher_id:hexid,authority_domain:authorityDomain,controller_identity:dbIdentity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,stage,stage_subject_sha256:sha256<StageSubjectV1>,owner_reservation_sha256:sha256<AssuranceResourceReservationV1>,owner_execution_sha256:sha256<ExecutionIdentityV1>,runtime_owner_sha256:sha256<RuntimeOwnerIdentityV1>,last_owner_heartbeat_at,last_owner_heartbeat_revision:u64>=1,lifecycle,opened_revision:u64>=1,terminal_revision?:u64,terminal_operation?:publisher_terminal_operation,recovery_proof_sha256?:sha256<PublisherRecoveryProofV1>`; `P-PUBLISHER-001` permits append only while ACTIVE; every append/finalize refreshes the exact owner heartbeat under the stage→publisher locks. `P-PUBLISHER-002` requires the terminal triple absent for ACTIVE, FINALIZED to carry `FINALIZE` without recovery proof, and ABANDONED to carry `ABANDON` plus proof. |
| `ArtifactPublisherOpenV1` / `artifact-publisher-open-v1` | `kind,schema_version,publisher_sha256:sha256<ArtifactPublisherReservationV1>,stage_subject_sha256:sha256<StageSubjectV1>,owner_reservation_sha256:sha256<AssuranceResourceReservationV1>,expected_stage_open_revision:u64>=1,opened_revision:u64>=1`; `P-PUBLISHER-003` requires one transaction to lock the stage row, prove OPEN/revision/subject/reservation equality, create-or-verify ACTIVE and increment revision. |

| `ArtifactPublisherFinalizeV1` / `artifact-publisher-finalize-v1` | `kind,schema_version,publisher_sha256:sha256<ArtifactPublisherReservationV1>,expected_stage_open_revision:u64>=1,expected_publisher_revision:u64>=1,finalized_revision:u64>=1,final_occurrence_sha256?:sha256<ArtifactOccurrenceV1>`; `P-PUBLISHER-005` requires the locked OPEN stage and ACTIVE publisher, optionally appends that exact final occurrence, then CASes ACTIVE→FINALIZED atomically. |
| `PublisherRecoveryProofV1` / `publisher-recovery-proof-v1` | `kind,schema_version,publisher_sha256:sha256<ArtifactPublisherReservationV1>,owner_reservation_sha256:sha256<AssuranceResourceReservationV1>,owner_reservation_revision:u64>=1,owner_execution_sha256:sha256<ExecutionIdentityV1>,runtime_owner_sha256:sha256<RuntimeOwnerIdentityV1>,owner_terminal_evidence_sha256?:sha256<ResourceEvidenceV1>,process_absence_sha256:sha256<ProcessAbsenceObservationV1>,cgroup_absence_sha256:sha256<CgroupAbsenceObservationV1>,container_absence_sha256?:sha256<ContainerAbsenceObservationV1>,last_owner_heartbeat_at,last_committed_occurrence_ordinal:u64,lease_expired_at,proof_assembled_at,proved_at_revision:u64>=1`; `P-PUBLISHER-006` requires the locked publisher/owner/reservation/key identities byte-equal; reservation is `SETTLING|RECOVERY_HOLD|RELEASED`; lease expiry precedes each observation; each observation is on the owner's exact host/worker, repeats the registered key's derived observer identity, is signed by that unexpired key, and names the exact PID/start/boot/cgroup/inode plus container when present. Each signed observation record is the canonical absence document; its probe-result digest binds the exact source bytes described in its row. Process precedes cgroup, which precedes optional container, adjacent observations differ by ≤5 seconds, all follow the last heartbeat, and `proof_assembled_at-observed_at(last)≤30 seconds`; the abandon transaction begins ≤30 seconds after assembly. Container observation is present iff owner has a container. The ordinal equals the locked maximum. A fresh host loads typed records/key from the domain store and verifies record canon, observer derivation, exact owner/probe/result digest, signatures and all ordering/freshness predicates before CAS; generic artifacts and controller assertions are invalid. Terminal evidence is required when already durable; the crash arm uses `RECOVERY_HOLD` and omits it, never fabricating evidence. |
| `ArtifactPublisherAbandonV1` / `artifact-publisher-abandon-v1` | `kind,schema_version,publisher_sha256:sha256<ArtifactPublisherReservationV1>,recovery_proof_sha256:sha256<PublisherRecoveryProofV1>,expected_stage_open_revision:u64>=1,expected_publisher_revision:u64>=1,abandoned_revision:u64>=1`; `P-PUBLISHER-007` requires the same stage→publisher lock order as append/finalize/close and CASes ACTIVE→ABANDONED only after exact proof; exact replay is read-only success. |

| `WorkerCapacityReservationV1` / `worker-capacity-reservation-v1` | `kind,schema_version,reservation_id:hexid,worker_identity:dbIdentity,authority_domain:authorityDomain,controller_identity:dbIdentity,repository_identity:identity,accepted_a_lineage_sha256:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,owner_invocation_ordinal:u64>=1,owner_cell_id:id,parent_reservation_sha256?:sha256<WorkerCapacityReservationV1>,parent_envelope_revision?:u64>=1,accounting_mode:{ROOT_CHARGE,INCLUSIVE_CHILD_SUBALLOCATION},allocation_profile_sha256:sha256<MaterializedResourceProfileV1>,direct_profile_sha256:sha256<MaterializedResourceProfileV1>,docker_daemon_capacity_sha256?:sha256<DockerDaemonCapacityV1>,lifecycle,reserved_at_revision:u64>=1,released_at_revision?:u64,release_evidence_sha256?:sha256<ResourceEvidenceV1>,process_memory_bytes:u64,process_pids:u64,process_cpu_micros_per_period:u64,process_cpu_period_micros:u64,nofile:u64,cache_bytes:u64,container_memory_bytes:u64,container_pids:u64,container_cpu_micros_per_period:u64,container_cpu_period_micros:u64,container_tmpfs_bytes:u64,container_shm_bytes:u64,container_log_bytes:u64,container_image_bytes:u64,docker_daemon_memory_bytes:u64,docker_daemon_pids:u64,docker_daemon_cpu_micros_per_period:u64,docker_daemon_cpu_period_micros:u64,docker_daemon_cache_bytes:u64,docker_daemon_log_bytes:u64,direct_process_memory_bytes:u64,direct_process_pids:u64,direct_process_cpu_micros_per_period:u64,direct_process_cpu_period_micros:u64,direct_nofile:u64,direct_cache_bytes:u64,direct_container_memory_bytes:u64,direct_container_pids:u64,direct_container_cpu_micros_per_period:u64,direct_container_cpu_period_micros:u64,direct_container_tmpfs_bytes:u64,direct_container_shm_bytes:u64,direct_container_log_bytes:u64,direct_container_image_bytes:u64,direct_docker_daemon_memory_bytes:u64,direct_docker_daemon_pids:u64,direct_docker_daemon_cpu_micros_per_period:u64,direct_docker_daemon_cpu_period_micros:u64,direct_docker_daemon_cache_bytes:u64,direct_docker_daemon_log_bytes:u64`; unprefixed counters are the immutable allocation envelope charged to the worker only for a root; `direct_*` counters are the portion reserved for this row's own execution. They equal their named profiles. Roots omit parent/revision and use ROOT_CHARGE; children require both and use INCLUSIVE_CHILD_SUBALLOCATION, and because depth 1 cannot parent, each child has direct profile/counters exactly equal allocation. Root direct counters may be smaller than allocation but never larger. First child atomically sets the separate locked database column `children_frozen_at_revision` and returns that value as `parent_frozen_revision`; the child copies it to `parent_envelope_revision`. The immutable parent canonical bytes/digest do not change, and thereafter its allocation, direct counters/profiles and revision-bound envelope cannot grow. Daemon digest is required iff any allocation daemon field is nonzero, `RELEASED` requires release fields, and `RECOVERY_HOLD` remains fully charged. This is `P-RESOURCE-002`. |
| `AssuranceResourceReservationV1` / `assurance-resource-reservation-v1` | `kind,schema_version,accepted_a_lineage_sha256:sha256<AcceptedALineageV1>,worker_identity:dbIdentity,authority_domain:authorityDomain,controller_identity:dbIdentity,repository_identity:identity,invocation_ordinal:u64>=1,cell_attempt_ordinal:u64>=1,cell_id:id,proof_id:id,stage,subject_sha256:sha256<StageSubjectV1>,validator_id:id,direct_materialized_resource_profile_id:id,direct_materialized_resource_profile_sha256:sha256<MaterializedResourceProfileV1>,allocation_materialized_resource_profile_sha256:sha256<MaterializedResourceProfileV1>,predecessor_state_revision:u64,predecessor_stage_tip_sha256:sha256<PhaseCheckpointV2>,reserved_worker_capacity_sha256:sha256<WorkerCapacityReservationV1>,parent_reservation_sha256?:sha256<AssuranceResourceReservationV1>,nesting_depth:u64,lifecycle`; `P-RESOURCE-003` requires validator and execution identity equal the direct profile, the workflow reservation equal the worker row's direct profile/counters, and the allocation digest equal its worker allocation profile. Ordinary roots/children have direct=allocation; only the frozen J-05 root may use the larger allocation envelope below. |
