The floor's required dimensions/evidence classes are exactly the arrays above; no caller may reduce them. The full parent reservation must be operationally usable and `RUNNING` before child activation. Both child workflow and worker reservations name their exact parent, use `INCLUSIVE_CHILD_SUBALLOCATION`, carry depth 1, contribute zero second worker-root charge, and consume the parent's dimension-by-dimension remaining allocation; two sibling reservations whose checked sum exceeds any parent dimension are rejected under the same worker lock. Parent AG-VAL-I-001 cannot start child A until all harmless `CODEX_BROKER_V1` probes PASS; failure is evidence-incomplete/validation-unavailable, never fallback to local Codex credentials.

## Functional-plan mutation and B convergence

The A model is immutable in B/C. The functional plan may only progress status/checkboxes or move semantically unchanged to completed. B convergence requires all B cells, including complete B secret inventory scan; C independently executes C cells and exact-head review.

V4 coordination order is exactly:

`DESIGN_ACCEPTED → IMPLEMENTATION_CONVERGED → BRANCH_ACCEPTED → FINAL_REVIEW_CLEAN → INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED → PR_SUBMITTING → (PR_PUBLISHED → MERGE_AUTHORIZED → MERGE_SUBMITTING → (MERGE_APPLIED → (POST_MERGE_ACCEPTED | POST_MERGE_FAILED) | MERGE_NOT_APPLIED | RECOVERY_REQUIRED) | PR_NOT_APPLIED | RECOVERY_REQUIRED)`. Only a valid `EffectReauthorizationV1` edge returns `PR_NOT_APPLIED→PR_SUBMITTING` or `MERGE_NOT_APPLIED→MERGE_SUBMITTING`; no other back edge exists. `RECOVERY_REQUIRED` may advance forward to the same ordinal's applicable APPLIED/NOT_APPLIED settlement only through the typed read-only reconciliation row above; it never issues a new capability. `CLAIMED_PRE_CALL`, `CALL_POSSIBLE`, `APPLIED`, `NOT_APPLIED`, `UNKNOWN` and `RECOVERY_REQUIRED` are the closed per-attempt states inside those coordination nodes.

### PR effect linearization

`internal/integrationgate` retains all existing integration preparation, textual/semantic validation, evidence publication and failure transitions, but on a clean result its final operation is the durable `INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED` event/decision. It must return that state and must not emit `READY_FOR_MERGE`; its former accepted→READY branch is removed. This is a bounded correction to ordering, not a weakening of integration checks.

After integration, the controller derives `PRPublicationAuthorityV1` from only the current `INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED` event, immutable integration/final-review tips, repository/candidate/baseline and exact create/update document. That pre-READY authority cannot name or hash READY, merge policy, expected merge content or retained merge authority. The controller then acquires the exact run-transition lease and seals `PREffectIntentV1` from a deterministic `PREffectRequestV1` plus one typed authentication context/mutation credential use. It installs the byte-bound V4 ledger barrier before the PostgreSQL claim race. PR invalidation versus claim competes on one PG predecessor tip while the barrier blocks every ledger transition except the exact authorized APPLIED readiness event bytes. Winner CAS installs `PREffectClaimV1`, updates the durable attempt index, sets `PR_SUBMITTING`, and returns both one-shot capabilities only to that winner. `internal/prlifecycle` accepts exact pre-READY authority+intent+claim+barrier+admission+authentication+credential capabilities and refuses HTTP otherwise. Claim loss before call admission uses `EffectPreCallClosureV1`; after admission, any response uses the atomic result/index transaction and only indexed-result absence permits the response-free anchor/read-only reconciliation chain.

Exact indexed PR APPLIED invokes the sole `MergeReadinessAuthorityV1` operation while the typed barrier and run lease remain active. It creates that authority and `ReadyForMergeEvidenceV1` from the immutable pre-READY publication authority, integration decision, combined-acceptance evidence, exact authorized PR request and exact authentication/credential-bound APPLIED observation/outcome. `AppendOrVerifyEffectTransitionV4` reconstructs the complete frozen preimage and appends only its byte-exact `INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED→READY_FOR_MERGE` event. The additive `internal/githublifecycle` bridge parses that evidence into retained `ReadyAuthorityBindingV1`/`ExpectedMergeContentV1`, then creates `GitHubMergeAuthorityAdapterV1` and the complete retained EP-005 merge authority; historical EP-005 V1 evidence retains its old validator and meaning. Only after ledger reopen verifies the exact bytes may PG settle `PR_PUBLISHED`. Exact NOT_APPLIED leaves the ledger at integration and only resolved settlement may authorize ordinal `n+1`; UNKNOWN retains barrier. A ledger-first READY crash settles PG from the indexed outcome and exact event; PG-first PR APPLIED is illegal.

### Merge and ledger↔PostgreSQL handoff

The append-only ledger remains the sole operational run-state truth (`INTEGRATION_ACCEPTED`, PR-derived `READY_FOR_MERGE`, terminal `MERGED`/`FAILED` etc.); PostgreSQL stores coordination/effect state only. `MergeEffectIntentV1` can exist only when the current ledger tip is exact `READY_FOR_MERGE`, its `ReadyForMergeEvidenceV1` and `GitHubMergeAuthorityAdapterV1` validate against exact indexed PR APPLIED semantics for the same run/candidate, and PG is `MERGE_AUTHORIZED`. Under the exact run-transition lease the controller installs a typed byte-bound barrier committed to that intent/event/effect-specific request/authentication/credential before the claim/index CAS sets `MERGE_SUBMITTING`. Only a confirmed `EffectCallAdmissionV1` consumed by both one-shot capabilities can enter `internal/mergelifecycle.Provider.SubmitTarget`; the authorized merge request must contain the exact existing `TargetSubmissionV1`.

If the indexed provider effect is APPLIED, merge lifecycle calls only `AppendOrVerifyEffectTransitionV4`; the ledger boundary reconstructs the complete preimage, validates typed authority and appends the exact `READY_FOR_MERGE→MERGED` bytes. Only after reopening/verifying those bytes may PG settle `MERGE_APPLIED`, typed-resolve/read-back the barrier, publish `EffectBarrierResolutionV1`, and later enter post-merge acceptance. Proven NOT_APPLIED settles `MERGE_NOT_APPLIED`, then typed-resolves and records the barrier, and may authorize only ordinal `n+1`; UNKNOWN enters `RECOVERY_REQUIRED` and retains the barrier. Claim without call admission is provably pre-call; admission with no indexed result permits only the competing-CAS response-free anchor and can infer nothing. A ledger-first crash settles PG from the indexed outcome and exact event; there is no legal PG-first APPLIED order and no second authoritative run state.

Post-merge acceptance failure leaves the factual merge/MERGED ledger event intact, stores `PostMergeFailureV1`, and sets PG coordination state `POST_MERGE_FAILED`; external-build readiness stays false. Any revert/repair is a new governed A/B/C change.

Publication/merge requires all applicable B/C/integration cells PASS, exact-head 0 Critical/0 Major, unchanged clean candidate, complete secret inventories, unexhausted budgets, operationally usable exact tips, typed credential/scope evidence and one-winner effect/credential capabilities. B scope includes the minimal required `internal/prlifecycle/**`, `internal/mergelifecycle/**`, `internal/integrationgate/**`, and `internal/githublifecycle/**`. The last two changes are bounded to stopping integration at accepted and additively admitting pre-READY `PRPublicationAuthorityV1` plus post-PR `ReadyForMergeEvidenceV1`/`GitHubMergeAuthorityAdapterV1`; historical EP-005 evidence, merge authorization and target-submission semantics cannot be weakened or reinterpreted.
